## Formalism Execution Requirements - 2026-05-24

The corrected goal requires a formalism-execution audit, not merely ACT syntax, smoke matrix, or
public render compliance. TTP activation and MRP emergence must be produced by daee formalism
itself and be auditable as state-transition grammar.

TTP execution proof requires:

```text
matched pressure
-> selected source-owned owner/TTP
-> owner operation
-> target pressure
-> mechanism/action
-> local state change Delta kappa / Delta nB
-> contribution-to-Land
-> Land(nB)
-> R(H,Delta)
```

MRP formal emergence proof requires:

```text
Land(nB)
-> Delta nB / Delta kappa
-> R(H,Delta)
-> route-gradient / ∇
-> ∇·T / ∇×T where licensed
-> MRP pressure read
-> resultant: held_burden_activation | generated_burden_instantiation |
   no_new_resultant | hold_partial | loopbreak | STOP
-> source-owned owner/TTP route
-> Layer B execution
-> Land
-> R(H,Delta) again
```

The checker and witness layers must not pass an output merely because it contains `[Mid-Reread
Pressure]`, `MRP(nB)`, a graph edge, a generated burden label, or a plausible resultant word. They
must prove the MRP section is downstream of Land, tied to Delta/R(H,Delta), has held/generated
provenance, re-enters owner routing, executes a Layer B owner body, and is mirrored in witness
surfaces.

Formalism-to-witness agreement is required for every burden cycle across:

- Layer B body
- ACT record
- MRP resultant
- Land line
- R(H,Delta)
- Closure/Reconstruction Witness
- `field_witness`
- Output Grapher graph_delta

If any surface claims a transition and another omits or contradicts it, the proof fails or becomes
HOLD/PARTIAL. If ∇, ∇·T, or ∇×T are only notation today, classify them as `OPEN FORMALISM TARGET -
not implemented proof` until source paths, checker rules, fixtures, and fresh outputs prove
otherwise.

Formalism audit lane:

- Name: `daee-formalism-emergence-auditor`
- Scope: audit whether TTP activation, MRP emergence, R(H,Delta), ∇, ∇·T, and ∇×T are executable
  state-transition grammar rather than rhetorical notation.
- Authority: read-only unless reassigned to a narrow patch surface; no release/package/tag/upload
  authority.
- Required report: `Formalism Emergence Audit` with verdict, TTP proof, MRP emergence proof,
  R(H,Delta), ∇, ∇·T, ∇×T, witness/Grapher agreement, decorative notation found, required patches,
  fixtures/canaries, and open rows.

## Architecture Finding: Per-Activation Proof Enables, But Does Not Equal, Graph-Level Collapse Proof

Status: `OPEN / TARGET GUARANTEE`

Dependency: `ACT Canonicalization / Compliance Stabilizer`

Classification:

- Useful architectural finding; not behavior proof.
- Not release readiness.
- Do not claim total collapse, graph completeness, NLA reconstructibility, progressive disclosure completeness, or field-wide reproducibility from per-activation canonicalization alone.

Layer distinction:

- Layer 1, per-activation verification: owner/TTP route selected; `body_ref` dereferences; body performs owner operation; pressure is targeted; `Δκ` / `ΔⁿB` is visible; Land contribution is licensed; `field_witness` mirrors activation.
- Layer 2, graph-level collapse verification: all diagnosed deformations represented as nodes; every node has verified activation or honest HOLD/PARTIAL; generated `𝔅_MRP` nodes are not backfilled into `𝔅_LA`; Land contributions chain correctly; recursion terminates honestly; no unresolved/orphan deformation remains hidden.

Correct conditional guarantee:

`For any input whose deformations are fully diagnosed by the rubric, and whose deformation nodes are all matched to catalogue-backed owner/TTP operations, the runtime should either produce a checker-verified, field_witness-mirrored, graphable collapse proof, or honestly return HOLD/PARTIAL with unmatched or unresolved nodes named.`

Classification row additions:

| Claim | Safe classification |
|---|---|
| Formal notation compliance | PARTIAL: improved by checker canonicalization; structurally closed only if model no longer authors authoritative notation or renderer derives notation from canonical state |
| Heuristic operator governance | PARTIAL: requires owner catalogue operation validation across fresh matrix |
| Per-activation reproducibility | NEEDS PROOF: requires repeated same-case runs showing stable canonical activations |
| NLA encode direction | FOUNDATION LAID: activation extraction can serve as encoder, but not complete |
| NLA decode direction | OPEN: needs explicit ACT/canonical object to natural-language reconstruction layer |
| Progressive skill disclosure | COMPATIBLE, NOT PROVEN: needs direct-routability proof and owner-load behavior |
| Graph-level collapse proof | OPEN: needs graph completeness checker |
| Total `𝔅` field reproducibility | OPEN: depends on graph layer and repeated-run isomorphism |
| field_witness proof certificate | PARTIAL: currently structural; target is graph-completeness certificate |

New backlog row:

`Graph-Level Collapse / field_witness Completeness`

Status: `OPEN — depends on stable per-activation canonicalization`

Required proof:

```text
diagnosed_set == graph.nodes.deformations
∀ node ∈ diagnosed_set:
    node has verified activation
    OR node has explicit HOLD/PARTIAL with reason

∀ activation:
    body_ref dereferences
    owner/TTP operation verified
    Δ verified
    Land contribution verified
    field_witness mirror exists

∀ generated burden:
    provenance is 𝔅_MRP
    not backfilled into 𝔅_LA

collapse_complete = true only if no unresolved node remains
```

Future checker direction:

- Suggested name: `tools\check_field_witness_graph_completeness.py`
- It should verify diagnosed deformation nodes, `𝔅_LA` / `𝔅_MRP` / `𝔅_total` union semantics, terminal status per burden, verified activation per LANDED node, generated-node provenance, no orphan nodes, honest recursion termination, and Closure Witness / field_witness / Output Grapher agreement.
- SVG / Output Grapher is a human-auditable render of the certificate, not proof by itself.

Overclaim guard:

- Do not say: "notation drift is fully solved", "operator governance is closed", "total noetic field collapse is proven", "field_witness guarantees collapse", "same input produces reproducible graph", "all deformations are diagnosed", or "owner catalogue is complete".
- Safe wording: "per-activation canonicalization is being stabilized"; "operator governance is checkable where owner/operation catalogue data exists"; "graph-level collapse proof is a target checker"; "field_witness can become a proof certificate after graph completeness is implemented"; "reproducibility requires repeated-run isomorphism proof"; "diagnostic completeness is a bounded rubric claim"; "unmatched nodes must produce HOLD/PARTIAL, not hallucinated closure".

Sequencing:

- Do not start graph-level collapse checker until narrow Trinitarian per-activation proof is green, strict ACT/MRP passes, field_witness mirror agreement passes, and at least one repeated-run stability check passes or is classified.

## Architecture Finding: Shannon / Integral Trace / field_witness Convergence

Status: `OPEN — ARCHITECTURAL FORMALIZATION TARGET`

Classification:

- Not implemented.
- Not release readiness.
- Do not claim total collapse, entropy reduction, graph convergence, or reproducibility from this framing.

Core insight:

```text
input noetic field
→ diagnosed burden set: 𝔅 = 𝔅_LA ∪ 𝔅_MRP
→ per-burden owner/TTP activation
→ checker-verified Δκ / ΔⁿB
→ licensed Land contribution
→ field_witness graph certificate
→ restorative public render
```

Safe formal framing:

```text
CollapseProof(Φ) =
  fold over 𝔅_total = 𝔅_LA ∪ 𝔅_MRP
  where each node contributes only if:
    owner/TTP route is catalogue-backed;
    body_ref dereferences;
    operation is performed;
    pressure is targeted;
    Δκ / ΔⁿB is visible;
    Land contribution is licensed;
    field_witness mirrors source/target/activation.
```

Research notation, provisional only:

```text
Collapse(Φ) ≈ Fold_{B ∈ 𝔅_total}(verified τ(B), Δκ(B), Land(B))
```

Do not claim `Collapse(Φ) = ∫_B τ(B) · Δκ(B) dB` until the repo defines `dB`, the measure/ordering of `Δκ`, `H(Φ)`, `H_fiṭrah`, and convergence checking.

Future backlog rows:

| Row | Status | Dependency |
|---|---|---|
| Shannon / encoding-decoding formalization | OPEN TARGET | ACT canonicalization |
| Integral trace / finite fold over burden graph | OPEN TARGET | graph completeness checker |
| field_witness convergence certificate | OPEN TARGET | owner_activation mirror + graph completeness |
| graph-level collapse proof | OPEN TARGET | per-activation proof stable |
| repeated-run graph reproducibility | OPEN TARGET | stable graph certificate + repeated smoke runs |
| Boltzmann Brain stress case | FUTURE SMOKE | graph-level checker |

Future checker direction:

- Suggested name: `tools\check_field_witness_convergence.py`
- It should verify diagnosed deformation coverage, `𝔅_LA`, `𝔅_MRP`, generated provenance, LANDED-node activations, unresolved-node HOLD/PARTIAL/STOP/non-load-bearing states, no orphan deformation nodes, honest termination, and Closure Witness / field_witness / Output Grapher agreement.

field_witness target role:

- Current: structural parser / reconstruction payload.
- Target: proof certificate over verified burden graph.
- Future invariant: `field_witness.collapse_complete = true` only if every diagnosed node has verified activation or explicit held/partial terminal state; otherwise `collapse_complete = false` and unresolved nodes are named.

Safe Shannon wording:

`The Shannon analogy is structurally useful because it clarifies why redundancy, canonicalization, and decoding checks matter. It does not by itself prove noetic collapse. It gives an engineering discipline: build encodings so drift becomes detectable inconsistency rather than plausible prose.`

Boltzmann Brain:

- Future stress case only, not current proof.
- Do not hardcode.
- Expected possible nodes include radical epistemic self-undermining, self-reference/coherence precondition, reliability-denial pressure, fiṭrah/baseline knowledge severance, and skepticism-carousel pressure.

Continue the current-SHA matrix refresh from generated skill SHA
`F30E718CB5CDCCDD5156093F670B3EE9CB7F76B79FB98BD0BCF74EBADA4A4DE9`.
Do not open graph-level / convergence checker work yet.

## Matrix Watchlist For Remaining Current-SHA Smokes

Use this as a validation watchlist only. Do not patch from it unless a validator failure or exact snippet proves the failure.

- Khaybar: source-order sorting; particular verdict vs general rule; Closing Formulation scoped boundary / reopen condition; generated `B_MRP` not backfilled into `B_LA`.
- Hujjah-hold: held route must either become a verified activation or remain HOLD/PARTIAL with reason; no permanent silent held route; hujjah condition must not collapse into generic closure.
- Secular moral realism: imported tribunal / philosophical-usurpation pressure; moral intuition not treated as autonomous proof without source-ground; owner operation label must be source-backed; Closing Formulation must not over-universalize.
- Doubt-churn: carousel/cycling pressure; distinguish broken carousel from still-cycling pressure; if still cycling, RECURSE/HOLD/PARTIAL must appear rather than fabricated closure.
- Sincere shubha / clarification pressure: classify as clarification/maieutic rather than hard worldview objection when appropriate; P4/P1-style clarification/restoration should be available; do not force reductio-heavy M1/M8 routing if the input is sincere confusion rather than polemical assertion.

## In-Scope Convergence Checker Design Guardrails

These rows were previously recorded as future design obligations. Under the corrected IMPLEMENTAUDIT
goal they are in-scope implementation targets, but they remain unimplemented and unproven. Do not
claim release readiness, total collapse, graph completeness, Shannon/integral proof, or NLA decode
closure from the current per-activation validator matrix.

- Current checkers are soundness checkers: claimed deformation/activation nodes are verified. They do not yet prove that all structurally live deformation nodes were diagnosed and claimed.
- The future `check_field_witness_convergence.py` should be both a soundness checker and a register-accounting completeness checker.
- Completeness source should start as `field_witness + Layer A register state`, not `field_witness` alone.
- If kappa is live, the graph must show a kappa-relevant burden, activation, HOLD/PARTIAL, or non-load-bearing proof.
- If authority/source-order pressure is live, the graph must show source-order/imported-tribunal/authority-order work or an explicit held reason.
- If RECURSE is live, closure cannot assert total collapse until recursive pressure is worked, held, or loop-broken.
- Case-family deformation floors may be added later as supplemental floors for known smoke families, but the first convergence design should not be case-family hardcoding.
- Deterministic owner activation ordering is the future solution path for exact activation fingerprint reproducibility: the Diagnostic IR should order owners per burden as required-before, parallel, contingent, optional/non-load-bearing, or HOLD/PARTIAL.
- NLA decode is an open target with an implementation path identified: decode CanonicalActivation through owner-catalogue lookup, then compare the reconstruction with dereferenced body_ref as a future semantic-faithfulness check.

## Output Grapher Formal Reread-State Agreement Slice - 2026-05-24

Classification: `PARTIAL_IMPLEMENTATION advanced: formalism-to-Output-Grapher agreement now checks field_witness.formal_reread_states[] when present; corrected goal still PARTIAL`.

Why this slice was next:

- The Formalism Emergence audit found that strict MRP validates `field_witness.formal_reread_states[]`, but Output Grapher reconstructibility did not inspect that formal state mirror.
- Without this slice, an output could be graph-reconstructible while its formal reread-state mirror disagreed with visible MRP route/result/graph state.
- This patch is limited to Output Grapher parser/checker fixtures. It does not package, release, tag, upload, move assets, edit release notes, or change runtime atomics.

Classification: `PARTIAL_IMPLEMENTATION advanced: deterministic owner activation ordering checker skeleton and drift canaries landed; corrected goal still PARTIAL`.

Implementation scope:

- Parses parser-stable embedded `field_witness` and requires `field_witness.owner_activations[]`.
- Reuses `check_mrp_generated_burden.py` owner/body-ref normalization helpers so owner families are catalogue-backed and `body_ref` belongs to the target burden.

- Treats `optional_non_load_bearing` and `hold_partial` as non-required rows for canonical required fingerprint comparison.
- Requires `ordering_group` for parallel/optional rows and `trigger` for contingent rows.
- Supports `field_witness.owner_activation_ordering.required_before[]` canaries and rejects inversions.
- Compares repeated-run coarse graph/topology fingerprints separately from canonical required owner activation fingerprints.

## Formal Reread-State Semantics Checker Slice - 2026-05-24

Classification: `PARTIAL_IMPLEMENTATION advanced: derived formal reread-state semantics checker landed; corrected goal still PARTIAL`.

Why this slice was next:

- Formalism Auditor A found that strict MRP and Output Grapher now compare the emitted formal state mirror, but route-gradient/divergence/curl remained mostly validated fields rather than derived route semantics.
- The earlier graph/register instruction said not to patch convergence directly. This slice therefore adds a standalone formal checker instead of changing `tools\check_field_witness_convergence.py`.
- This patch is checker/fixture-only. Runtime atomics/build, Grapher rendering, manual render, package, release notes, provenance, tags, uploads, and release assets were not touched.

Files added:

- `tools\check_formal_reread_state_semantics.py`
- `tests\formal-reread-state-semantics\valid\held-generated-stop.md`
- `tests\formal-reread-state-semantics\invalid\generated-target-absent-from-b-mrp.md`
- `tests\formal-reread-state-semantics\invalid\held-target-absent-from-b-la.md`
- `tests\formal-reread-state-semantics\invalid\loopbreak-missing-payload.md`
- `tests\formal-reread-state-semantics\invalid\stop-curl-non-null.md`
- `tests\formal-reread-state-semantics\invalid\stop-divergence-non-neutral.md`

Implementation scope:

- Parses embedded parser-stable `field_witness` and requires non-empty `formal_reread_states[]`.
- Reuses existing `closure_witness_lib.py` and `check_mrp_generated_burden.py` normalization helpers.
- Requires formal state fields for source burden, prior Land, Delta, `R(H,Delta)`, route-gradient, divergence, curl, route result type, MRP resultant, graph delta, preemption basis, and route.
- Derives held route semantics: `held_burden_activation` must route to a `B_LA` burden, must not target generated `B_MRP`, must have graph edge coverage, and must carry an owner route.
- Derives generated route semantics: `generated_burden_instantiation` must route to `B_MRP`, must match `generated_burdens.generated_by=MRP(source)`, must set `generated_by`, must have graph edge coverage, and must mark the target as generated/new/absent from `B_LA`.
- Derives STOP semantics: `no_new_resultant` must use route `STOP`, graph delta `none`, no `next_burden`, neutral divergence, and null/resolved curl unless explicit HOLD/PARTIAL/LoopBreak accounting exists.
- Derives LoopBreak floor: a LoopBreak claim must include `loopbreak_target`, `loopbreak_ground`, `loopbreak_delta`, and `post_break_reread`.
- Compares formal states to `mrp_resultants[]` and `reread_records[]` so formal state cannot drift from machine-readable route/result/release records.

## Shannon Finite-Fold Trace Checker Slice - 2026-05-24

Classification: `PARTIAL_IMPLEMENTATION advanced: concrete finite-fold trace checker landed; literal Shannon/integral theorem remains research-only`.

Why this slice was next:

- Subagent E found that the safe implementation path is a finite fold over `B_total = B_LA ∪ B_MRP`, not a literal entropy or integral theorem.
- This checker turns the implementable piece into a gate: terminal accounting for every burden, Land/activation evidence for landed burdens, generated-node provenance for `B_MRP`, and explicit rejection of literal entropy/integral proof claims.
- This patch is checker/fixture-only. Runtime atomics/build, package, release notes, provenance, tags, uploads, and release assets were not touched.

Implementation scope:

- Parses embedded parser-stable `field_witness`.
- Enforces `B_total` graph discipline through existing `field_witness_graph_errors()`.
- Checks every `B_total` node has terminal accounting.
- Requires landed nodes to have `field_witness.owner_activations[]` target evidence with concrete owner, operation, pressure, `body_ref`, delta, and Land target.
- Accepts honest HOLD/PARTIAL only when terminal detail names the remaining pressure/reason and complete closure is not claimed.
- Requires each `B_MRP` node to have `generated_burdens` provenance from `MRP(source)`, matching `generated_burden_instantiation` resultant, and coverage graph edge.
- Requires complete finite fold to have neutral divergence and null/resolved curl.
- Rejects literal proof claims involving truth/meaning/warrant entropy, lower-entropy proof, literal Shannon theorem, literal integral theorem, `Collapse(Phi)=integral`, undefined `H(Phi)`, undefined `H_fitrah`, or undefined `dB` proof language.

## Register-Derived Burden Floor Generalization - 2026-05-24

Classification: `PARTIAL_IMPLEMENTATION advanced: register-to-burden obligation rule, Trinitarian canary, non-Trinitarian fixture proof, and negative canaries landed; live non-Trinitarian smoke remains open`.

Stop condition:

- Do not patch repeated-run stability directly in this slice.
- Do not package, tag, upload, edit release notes, or begin release/provenance work.
- Do not mark the burden-floor generalization row `DONE` until a non-Trinitarian live smoke, or an explicitly accepted fixture-only closure decision, proves the same live-register floor mechanism outside the Trinitarian John 17:3 canary.

Implementation:

- Added `register_burden_floor` fixture-matrix coverage to `tools\check_register_formalism_bridge.py`.
- Added a register-to-burden obligation validator:
  - `Omega` live requires an ontological/predication burden.
  - `xi` live requires a warrant/authority burden.
  - `mu` live requires a memetic-carrier decomposition burden.
  - `kappa` live requires a dependency/collapse burden.
  - `heart` live requires an affective/posture burden.
  - Burdens may be multi-typed; coverage is per live register, not one burden per register.
- The checker rejects burden floors without `live_registers(IR(...))` derivation and rejects hardcoded/case-template derivations.
- Updated Diagnostic IR/runtime guidance so burden-floor structure is derived from live registers in `IR(N,m,tau,sigma,heart,xi,Omega,mu,kappa)`. The Trinitarian John 17:3 B labels are now framed as canary-instance labels supplied by the Trinitarian N-frame, not as a general runtime template.
- Rebuilt generated runtime and generated docs index after normal freshness checks required it.

Fixtures:

- Positive: `tests\register-formalism-bridge-fixtures\14-register-derived-burden-floor-trinitarian.json`.
  - Proves `IR(N=Trinitarian)` with live `{Omega, xi, mu, kappa}` yields register-typed floor coverage.
  - Uses a multi-typed burden for `Omega` + `mu` to prove coverage does not require one burden per register.
- Positive: `tests\register-formalism-bridge-fixtures\17-register-derived-burden-floor-naturalism.json`.
  - Proves a non-Trinitarian N-frame with live `{xi, mu, Omega, kappa}` yields register-typed floor coverage.
  - Uses the naturalism/default-carrier case to show the floor mechanism is not defined by John 17:3 labels.
- Negative: `tests\register-formalism-bridge-fixtures\15-register-burden-floor-missing-omega-canary.json`.
  - Proves a live `Omega` register cannot pass with only a `xi`-typed burden.
- Negative: `tests\register-formalism-bridge-fixtures\16-register-burden-floor-hardcoded-template-canary.json`.
  - Proves a John 17:3 B1-B5 exact-label list without live-register derivation is a derivation failure, even if the labels look familiar.
